At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [174] [Align-RAG: Alignment Is All You Need for TSFM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05571)
*Mohammad Asadi,Soheil Hor,Bardiya Akhbari,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Tahoura Nedaee,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Align-RAG是一种无需训练的检索增强预测方法，通过闭式幅度重缩放和相位偏移对齐检索样本，使冻结的时间序列基础模型能动态利用上下文，在多个基准上超越有监督的融合模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强预测方法假设冻结骨干网络无法自主利用检索上下文，因此引入可学习融合模块；本文旨在验证该假设是否必要，并探索无需训练的对齐策略是否能释放冻结模型的上下文学习能力。

Method: 提出Align-RAG：对检索到的历史-未来窗口对施加闭式逐对幅度重缩放和整数滞后相位偏移，直接送入冻结骨干网络上下文，无任何可学习参数。

Result: 在冻结Chronos-Bolt上，Align-RAG在七个标准基准数据集上平均MSE降低3.75%，超越最先进的训练融合适配器；在四个不同架构的冻结TSFM上零样本MSE提升2.5%-13.7%。对齐演示引起的预测偏移与同对闭式岭回归预测偏移一致，未来打乱控制排除了简单平均未来的解释。

Conclusion: 冻结时间序列基础模型已具备动态上下文学习检索样本的能力；闭式对齐应成为检索增强预测的默认基线，在训练任何融合模块前优先考虑。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented forecasting promises to adapt frozen Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to new domains without fine-tuning, but recent methods typically rely on learned fusion modules, i.e., trained adapters that merge retrieved examples into the backbone's forecast, based on the assumption that frozen backbones cannot dynamically incorporate retrieved context on their own. We show this assumption is unnecessary. We introduce Align-RAG, a training-free method that applies a closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling and integer-lag phase shift to retrieved past-future windows before they enter a frozen backbone's context. With no learned parameters, Align-RAG outperforms the state-of-the-art trained retrieval adapter on a frozen Chronos-Bolt on all seven datasets of the standard benchmark (avg -3.75% MSE), showing that the gains previously attributed to learned fusion are recoverable without any training. Align-RAG further improves zero-shot MSE on four additional frozen TSFMs with various architectures by 2.5% to 13.7% per backbone with no per-backbone tuning. To probe why alignment helps, we compare the frozen backbone's prediction shift under aligned demonstrations to the closed-form ridge prediction shift on the same pairs. We find that aligned demonstrations induce prediction shifts that track a closed-form ridge predictor on the same pairs, with a future-shuffle control ruling out a futures-averaging account. Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [175] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 量子结构世界模型（QSWM）是一种受量子理论启发的预测性世界建模框架，引入复值表示和密度矩阵式潜在状态，在元胞自动机任务上显示出局部预测潜力，但在长时程推演和密度矩阵变体上存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子理论中的数学结构（如复值表示、密度矩阵式潜在状态）是否能为世界建模提供有用的归纳偏置，解决现有世界模型在结构化潜在状态、转移算子和解码映射上的局限。

Method: 提出量子结构世界模型（QSWM）框架，包含结构化潜在状态、潜在转移算子和受测量启发的解码映射；建立经典包含性、预测充分性和结构紧凑性三大基础性质；实例化复值QSWM和密度矩阵式QSWM两种变体，在初等元胞自动机上与强经典基线对比评估。

Result: 复值QSWM展现出良好的局部预测潜力；但长时程推演存在局限，密度矩阵式变体表现不如预期。

Conclusion: 量子启发的结构化表示为世界建模提供了有前景的归纳偏置，特别是在局部预测方面，但需进一步改进长时程建模能力和密度矩阵式变体的有效性。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [176] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可证明的流水线，通过凸学习和可证明的蒸馏，从非线性动力系统中提取紧凑的线性状态空间表示，无需直接求解非凸系统辨识问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决非线性动力系统学习中避免非凸优化的问题，通过凸方法学习隐式预测器，再蒸馏为显式线性动力系统，实现端到端的可证明保证。

Method: 1) 使用观测谱滤波（OSF）这一凸方法学习隐式谱预测器，与最佳线性观测器竞争；2) 应用谱到LDS蒸馏，将预测器转换为显式循环线性动力系统。

Result: 主定理表明蒸馏后的LDS平均预测误差分解为指数级小的蒸馏项和由Luenberger复杂度控制的OSF学习项；保证与维度无关，仅依赖观测器复杂度。实验在线性LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆上验证，蒸馏后的紧凑LDS预测器匹配或优于直接训练的基线。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了通过凸学习后跟可证明蒸馏，端到端地提取非线性动力学的事后最佳LDS表示，为非线性系统辨识提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [177] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需群组标注的方法，利用收敛后模型对固定扰动的敏感性差异识别受虚假相关影响的样本，并通过重采样提升最差群组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别虚假相关受影响样本的方法依赖早期训练信号，需借助群组标注验证集选择干预轮数，存在超参数敏感且需标注数据的局限。

Method: 在模型收敛后，对每个训练样本施加固定输入扰动，进行两次前向传播；若预测翻转则判定为受虚假相关影响样本。利用检测出的样本重平衡训练分布，无需群组标注、无需早停轮数。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，该方法将最差群组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，接近使用真实群组标签的85.8%上限。

Conclusion: 收敛模型对扰动的脆弱性差异可作为可靠信号识别虚假相关样本，提供了一种免标注、免超参的实用解决方案，显著缩小了与有监督方法的性能差距。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [178] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将对抗流网络(AFlowNets)扩展到不完全信息博弈，提出了信息流网络(IFNs)。作者证明了此前在完全信息博弈中建立的生成流网络约束不适用于获取有效密度(对应玩家策略)和有效训练目标，而IFNs解决了这一问题并严格泛化了AFlowNets。在三个标准博弈环境的初步实验中，IFNs在性能和速度上均与结果采样蒙特卡洛反事实遗憾最小化(OSMCCFR)和标准强化学习方法相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管许多算法将强化学习(RL)与反事实遗憾最小化(CFR)方法结合以利用计算速度和性能的权衡，但针对不完全信息博弈中生成采样框架的博弈论应用研究较少。本文旨在填补这一空白，将AFlowNets扩展到不完全信息博弈设置。

Method: 作者将对抗流网络(AFlowNets)扩展到不完全信息博弈，提出了信息流网络(IFNs)。他们证明了此前在完全信息博弈中为生成流网络建立的约束条件，对于获得有效密度(对应玩家策略)和有效训练目标是不可接受的。IFNs缓解了这一问题，并严格泛化了AFlowNets。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境的初步实验结果中，IFNs在性能和速度方面均与结果采样蒙特卡洛反事实遗憾最小化(OSMCCFR)和标准强化学习方法表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: 所提出的IFNs框架成功将生成流网络扩展到不完全信息博弈，提供了AFlowNets的有效泛化，并在性能上能与OSMCCFR和强化学习方法等成熟方法竞争。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [179] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视探索式建模（XM）中的“第三预训练轴”，证明其核心并非生成表达性，而是自由度（行为约束的弱度）。理论推导表明XM平均损失取决于候选遗漏可接受区域的概率，探索将遗漏概率提升到K次方；当K>1时，匹配概率随自由度增加。实验证实XM优化自由度：更大的K提升或饱和自由度，在上下文相关目标下每个测试值均增加自由度。自由度选择器在30次对比中29次优于验证选择。生成表达性仅是自由度的模式计数代理，丢弃了赋予自由度泛化意义的扩展结构。XM是手段，自由度是目的，选择自由度改善了分布偏移下的XM表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将探索式建模（XM）的“第三预训练轴”归因于生成表达性，但作者指出自由度（行为约束的弱度）才是本质。此前研究已证明自由度是函数而非形式的属性，且最弱模型最易泛化，自由度选择在归纳实验中比最小描述长度（MDL）高出110-500%。本文旨在形式化证明XM损失与自由度的关系，并实证验证XM实际优化的是自由度而非表达性。

Method: 1. 理论分析：推导XM平均损失取决于候选遗漏可接受区域的概率，探索将该概率提升到K次方，证明K>1时匹配概率随自由度单调上升。2. 前向XM实验：训练不同K值的XM候选池，测量自由度随K的变化。3. 选择器对比：在无标签父上下文上训练自由度选择器，与验证集选择进行30次对比实验。4. 分布偏移测试：评估自由度选择在分布偏移下对XM的改进效果。

Result: 1. 理论：XM平均损失由遗漏概率的K次方决定，K>1时更高自由度带来更高匹配概率。2. 实证：更大K增加或饱和测量自由度，在上下文相关目标下每个测试K值均提升自由度。3. 自由度选择器在30次对比中29次击败验证选择。4. 生成表达性仅为自由度的模式计数代理，丢失泛化关键的扩展结构。5. 选择自由度显著改善分布偏移下的XM性能。

Conclusion: 探索式建模的“第三轴”实为自由度而非生成表达性。XM通过多候选探索隐式优化自由度，但显式选择自由度（利用无标签上下文）比验证选择更有效。自由度作为函数属性而非形式属性，其扩展结构决定泛化能力；生成表达性仅是粗糙代理。未来工作应直接优化自由度而非表达性，以提升泛化和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [180] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoHarness-RL 通过离线学习 harness 策略并在线部署，利用监督微调和成本感知 GRPO 训练智能体构建和协调外部 harness 状态（BPE），在 ALFWorld 上达到 96.9% 成功率，展示了 harness 退火和演化动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 LLM 智能体依赖外部执行支持（harness）维护状态、跟踪进度、调用工具、验证结果和复用经验，但现有方法通过提示、启发式或领域约定手工设计 harness 工作空间及其使用策略，面临从噪声交互轨迹形成状态和运行时控制外部状态访问的挑战。

Method: 提出 EvoHarness-RL，将 Belief、Progress、Experience（BPE）作为面向策略的 harness 状态；先用监督 harness 微调教会基础智能体 harness 动作空间及构建有用外部状态；再用成本感知 GRPO 探索协调策略，在长时程交互中选择性读取、更新和巩固状态。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上基于 Qwen3-8B 实例化，达到 96.9% 成功率；观察到两个关键动态：harness annealing（训练将常见 harness 使用模式内化为模型策略，从频繁 harness 调用转向选择性外部状态访问）和 harness evolution（进度更新和经验巩固将 harness 优化为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基质）。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体受益于可训练的策略来构建和协调外部 harness 工作空间，而非仅靠增加更强工具或更大记忆。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [181] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出混合概率zonope（HProbZ）输出头，将预测不确定性分解为离散模式选择、有界系统漂移和不可约随机噪声三个来源，实现闭式似然、步间代数耦合、可识别性理论及多模态共形预测，实验显示优于同编码器混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络概率预测头要么输出高斯混合，要么输出单一共形区域，无法同时分离真实预测任务中常见的三种不同不确定性来源：模式间的离散选择、选定模式内的有界系统漂移、以及不可约的随机噪声。

Method: 引入混合概率zonope（HProbZ）输出头，用zonope的二元生成器、有界生成器和随机生成器分别表示上述三类不确定性，通过卷积获得闭式似然；在预测步骤间共享有界生成器，实现未来预测的代数耦合，使观测单步即可在一次前向传播中细化所有剩余步的预测分布。

Result: 理论上证明三类生成器可从似然中识别（至多排列），HProbZ密度在表示上区别于任何有限高斯混合；推理时同一共享结构提供解析式逐模式风险和免分布多模态共形集；实验在代表性预测基准上验证了相对同编码器混合基线的有效性，并同时具备混合模型和凸共形预测器不共同拥有的结构性质。

Conclusion: HProbZ通过zonope生成器的混合结构，统一了多模态不确定性建模、步间信息共享、理论可识别性和分布自由共形推断，为序列概率预测提供了一个具有独特结构优势的新范式。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [182] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了变换自由度(TDOF)来量化集合目标的复杂度，证明了标准多头注意力因值投影固定而需要深度与TDOF成正比，而提出的矩阵带状注意力(MZAttn)通过上下文自适应的矩阵带状族在单层即可表示同一目标，实验验证了该优势在高秩稀疏组合目标上显著，在聚合统计目标上不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标准多头注意力中输入相关的softmax路由与输入无关的线性值投影的不对称性对排列不变集合目标的影响，量化目标算子的复杂度并寻求更高效的表示方式。

Method: 提出变换自由度(TDOF)作为复杂度度量，进行深度分离分析；设计矩阵带状注意力(MZAttn)，用中心矩阵加生成器矩阵按输入相关门控加权求和替代固定值投影，保持排列等变性且初始化时退化为标准多头注意力。

Result: 理论证明上下文刚性注意力需要与TDOF成正比的深度，而MZAttn单层即可表示；实验显示MZAttn在高秩稀疏组合集合预测任务上优势明显，在聚合统计任务上与参数量匹配的标准注意力相当。

Conclusion: MZAttn通过引入上下文自适应的值投影族，在保持排列等变性的前提下显著降低了表示高复杂度集合目标所需的深度，其优势选择性地体现在目标依赖输入集合的高秩稀疏组合结构时。

Abstract: Multi-head attention combines an input-dependent softmax routing with an input-independent linear value projection, so the per-sample operator mapping aggregated values to outputs is the same for every input set. We study the consequences of this asymmetry for permutation-invariant set targets. We introduce the Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF) of a target operator, a complexity measure counting the input-dependent directions an exact representation requires, and present a depth-separation analysis showing that context-rigid attention needs depth proportional to the target's TDOF, whereas a single layer with a context-adaptive value family can represent the same target. Building on this analysis, we propose Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn), which replaces the fixed value projection with a context-adaptive matrix-zonotope family: a centre matrix plus a sum of generator matrices weighted by input-dependent gates. The construction reduces to standard multi-head attention at initialisation, preserves permutation equivariance, and admits a data-driven reachability interpretation. Experiments on a range of set-prediction tasks are consistent with the TDOF prediction that the architectural advantage is selective: it appears on targets that depend on the input set in a high-rank, sparsely combinatorial way, and is small on aggregate-statistic targets where parameter-matched standard attention is already competitive.

</details>


### [183] [KV-Skill: Forging Expertise in the Model's Native Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05475)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Bing Han,Kai Liu,Danqi Hu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KV-Skill，一种外部因子化算子设计空间，冻结语言模型通过轻量级接口读取。提供两条互补路径：注册（将文本技能转为文本衍生算子并训练共享骨干接口）和奖励学习（直接从任务结果学习紧凑潜在算子）。实验表明KV-Skill在十个基准和四个骨干模型上显著优于基线，文本衍生算子仅需每层一个任务对齐方向即可保留几乎全部收益，且共享接口可加载三个独立KV-Skill而无灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务知识存储方式存在局限：提示词文本模块化但每次使用需解释；权重适应使能力难以独立加载、移除或共享。需要一种外部算子机制，兼具模块化、可复用、不增加提示词长度，且能从文本或经验获取知识。

Method: KV-Skill包含两条路径：1) 注册路径：将人工编写的文本技能转换为文本衍生算子，并训练共享的每骨干接口；2) 奖励学习路径：直接从任务结果（有或无人工技能）学习紧凑潜在算子。两者均不向提示词添加位置。算子以因子化形式外挂，通过轻量级接口注入冻结骨干模型。

Result: 在十个基准和四个骨干（三个模型族）上，KV-Skill持续提升程序性知识效果。Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath上注册路径达77.2准确率，远高于源文本技能23.4、SkillOpt 52.0、SoftSkill 64.5。匹配奖励训练和参数预算下，KV-Skill在八个对比设置中七个优于软前缀、前缀微调和LoRA。事后秩分析显示文本衍生算子仅需每注入层一个任务对齐方向即保留近乎全部收益，随机方向则失效。共享接口可同时保留三个独立可加载的KV-Skill，无可测量遗忘。

Conclusion: 任务知识可从文本或经验获取，压缩为外部算子，并独立于骨干模型部署。KV-Skill实现了知识的模块化、可复用、可组合存储与加载，为冻结大模型的技能扩展提供了高效范式。

Abstract: Task knowledge is commonly stored either as text in the prompt or as an update to model weights. Text is modular but must be interpreted on every use, while weight adaptation makes the resulting capability difficult to load, remove, or share independently. We introduce KV-Skill, a design space of external factorized operators that a frozen language model reads through a lightweight interface. KV-Skill supports two complementary paths. Registration converts an authored text skill into a text-derived operator and trains a shared per-backbone interface. Reward learning develops a compact latent operator directly from task outcomes, with or without an authored skill. Neither path adds positions to the prompt. Across ten benchmarks and four backbones from three model families, converting text to a KV-Skill consistently makes the same procedural knowledge more effective. On Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath, registration reaches 77.2 accuracy, compared with 23.4 for the source text skill, 52.0 for SkillOpt, and 64.5 for SoftSkill. Under matched reward training and parameter budgets, KV-Skill gives the best result in seven of eight matched settings against soft prefixes, prefix tuning, and LoRA. A post-hoc rank analysis further shows that text-derived operators retain nearly all of their benefit with one task-aligned direction per injection layer, while matched random directions fail. Finally, one shared interface retains three independently loadable KV-Skills without measurable forgetting. These results show that task knowledge can be acquired from text or experience, compressed into an external operator, and deployed separately from the backbone. Code is available at: https://github.com/shawnzhg/KV-Skill

</details>


### [184] [LC-GRPO: Bridging Train-Inference Gap for Flow-Based GRPO with Langevin Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05600)
*Yingqing Guo,Hui Yuan,Zijian He,Mengdi Wang,Zheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LC-GRPO，一种基于流模型的 GRPO 框架，通过 Langevin 修正将确定性 ODE 推理步与随机探索结合，解决训练时 SDE 采样与测试时 ODE 采样的分布错配问题，在文生图/视频任务上显著提升奖励优化效果并保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式生成模型通常用确定性 ODE 采样，但在线强化学习需要随机 rollout 进行策略探索。现有 GRPO 方法在训练时用 SDE 替代 ODE，但有限步离散化下两者分布差异大：随着探索噪声增加，SDE rollout 变得模糊，导致训练样本与测试时 ODE 生成样本不匹配，影响策略优化效果。

Method: LC-GRPO 每步 rollout 先执行一个与推理对齐的 ODE Euler 步，再施加一个针对该时刻边缘分布的随机 Langevin 修正。所需 score 直接从流速度场恢复，无需额外 score 模型；转移核保持各向同性高斯，似然可计算，便于策略优化。理论上证明：在适当条件下，一次 Langevin 修正能降低不完美 ODE Euler 步的 Wasserstein 误差；在相同随机性水平下，该转移比逆向 SDE 的 Euler-Maruyama 离散化更准确。

Result: 在 SD3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-Dev 和 HunyuanVideo 上实验表明，LC-GRPO 在文生图和文生视频任务上持续提升奖励优化性能，保持生成质量，并大幅缩小随机训练 rollout 与确定性测试 ODE 推理之间的分布差距。

Conclusion: LC-GRPO 通过 Langevin 修正将确定性 ODE 推理与随机探索有效融合，在理论上保证了有限步离散化的准确性，在实践中显著改善了流模型在线强化学习的训练-推理一致性，为基于流模型的生成式 RL 提供了更 principled 的解决方案。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are typically sampled by solving a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), whereas online reinforcement learning requires stochastic rollouts for policy exploration and optimization. Existing GRPO methods for flow models therefore replace the inference-time ODE with a stochastic differential equation (SDE) during training. Although the ODE and SDE share the same marginal distributions in continuous time, their finite-step discretizations can differ substantially. In particular, SDE rollouts often become blurry as the exploration noise increases, creating a mismatch between the samples used for reinforcement learning and those generated by the test-time ODE sampler. We introduce LC-GRPO, a flow-based GRPO framework with Langevin correction. Each rollout transition first takes an inference-aligned ODE Euler step and then applies a stochastic Langevin correction targeting the marginal distribution at the resulting timestep. The required score is recovered directly from the flow velocity, requiring no additional score model, while the resulting transition remains an isotropic Gaussian with a tractable likelihood for policy optimization. We theoretically show that, under suitable conditions, one Langevin correction step reduces the Wasserstein error of an imperfect ODE Euler step. At a matched randomness level, we further show that the proposed transition can be more accurate than the standard Euler--Maruyama discretization of the reverse SDE. Experiments on SD3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-Dev, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LC-GRPO consistently improves reward optimization across text-to-image and text-to-video tasks, preserves generation quality, and substantially narrows the gap between stochastic training rollouts and deterministic test-time ODE inference.

</details>


### [185] [GAUGE: Granularity-Adaptive Counterfactual Gating of Evidence for Incomplete Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05608)
*Yunping Shi,En Yu,Kairui Guo,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAUGE是一个轻量级的反事实门控框架，用于不完整多模态分类，通过细粒度证据单元和泰勒证据分数实现单元级证据调制，在六个基准上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态分类方法假设所有模态可用，但真实场景常面临模态缺失；现有补全和动态融合方法在粗粒度模态层面操作，无法在同一恢复模态内保留可靠成分并抑制误导性成分，损害预测可靠性。

Method: GAUGE使用冻结插补器填补缺失模态，将观测和恢复输入统一编码为细粒度证据单元；通过预测感知的泰勒证据分数在一次前向反向传递中评估每个单元被参考表示替换的反事实效应；将分数映射为连续门控，转换为加性注意力logit偏置，实现单元级证据调制且不改变骨干网络架构。

Result: 在六个基准实验中，GAUGE在多样化的不完整输入设置下优于强基线；泰勒余项理论分析刻画了一阶近似相对于精确反事实效应的误差。

Conclusion: GAUGE建立了一个在模态不完整条件下进行细粒度证据控制的原则性且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Multimodal classification typically assumes all modalities are available, yet real-world inputs are often incomplete. Imputation and dynamic fusion can mitigate such incompleteness, but existing methods operate at a coarse modality level and thus cannot retain reliable components while suppressing misleading ones within the same recovered modality, compromising prediction reliability. To address this issue, we propose GAUGE, a lightweight counterfactual gating framework for incomplete multimodal classification. GAUGE first imputes missing modalities with a frozen imputer and encodes observed and recovered inputs uniformly as fine-grained evidence units. Rather than intervening on each unit explicitly, GAUGE scores the counterfactual effect of replacing every unit with a reference representation through prediction-aware Taylor evidence scores, all obtained in a single forward-backward pass. These scores are mapped to continuous gates, which are converted into additive attention-logit biases for unit-wise evidence modulation without altering the backbone architecture. Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that GAUGE outperforms strong baselines across diverse incomplete-input settings. Furthermore, a Taylor remainder theoretical analysis characterizes the error of the first-order approximation relative to the exact counterfactual effect, establishing GAUGE as a principled and scalable framework for fine-grained evidence control under modality incompleteness.

</details>


### [186] [Reasoning Errors Have a Region and a Direction in the Residual-Stream Trajectory of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05660)
*Hamed Damirchi,Ignacio Meza De la Jara,Damith Ranasinghe,Yuhang Liu,Javen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出三流检测器，结合残差流位移（运动）与粗粒度区域读取器（向量量化）和细粒度方向读取器（归一化多层状态），在未见推理基准上比位移-only SOTA 提升 12%、比单层探测基线提升 21%，并泛化到事实补全与事实核验任务，超越所有对比检测器。消融显示运动、区域、方向互补，表明推理有效性应从状态条件运动而非静态状态或去语境化轨迹中读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型越多用于需可验证推理的任务，可靠区分合理推理与错误推理成为关键问题。现有基于轨迹的方法利用逐层残差流位移捕捉表示变化，但位移省略了更新起始的状态，而恢复完整状态又可能重引入易走捷径的信息，二者存在权衡。

Method: 提出三流检测器：(1) 运动流——层间残差流位移；(2) 粗粒度区域读取器——基于向量量化的离散区域编码；(3) 细粒度方向读取器——基于归一化多层状态的方向信息。两个受限视图提供状态上下文，避免完全状态探测引入的捷径偏差。

Result: 在训练时未见的推理基准上，选择准确率比仅用位移的 SOTA 高达 +12%，比单层探测基线高达 +21%。虽仅在推理基准训练，也能零样本迁移到事实补全与事实核验，超越所有对比检测器。消融实验证实运动、区域、方向三流信号互补。

Conclusion: 推理有效性更适合从状态条件运动（state-conditioned motion）中读取，而非仅依赖静态状态或去语境化的轨迹。三流设计在保留状态上下文与避免捷径信息之间取得了更好平衡。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly used for tasks that require verifiable reasoning, reliably distinguishing sound reasoning from flawed reasoning has become an important practical problem. Recent trajectory-based methods seek this signal in layerwise residual-stream displacements, which capture how representations change while attenuating some stable, token-specific information. However, displacement omits the state from which an update originates, whereas restoring the full state risks reintroducing shortcut-prone information. We identify this trade-off and propose a three-stream detector that combines motion with two restricted views of location. A coarse region reader based on vector quantization and a fine direction reader over normalized multi-layer states. This design restores enough state context to interpret the motion without returning to full-state probing. On reasoning benchmarks unseen during training, our method improves selection accuracy by up to 12% over the displacement-only state of the art and 21% over single-layer probing baselines. Although trained only on reasoning benchmarks, it also reads factual completion and fact verification, ahead of every detector we compare against, which places the signal on correctness rather than on a kind of reasoning. Ablations further show that motion, region, and direction provide complementary signals. These results suggest that reasoning validity is better read from state-conditioned motion than from either static states or decontextualized trajectories alone.

</details>


### [187] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了势匹配最优传输（PMOT），一种用于通用 p-代价最优传输的势流框架，通过标量势参数化 CNF 速度场，利用自感应匹配损失训练，实现零损失精确性，能恢复 p-最优传输映射和动力学，在合成基准、高维表格数据密度建模和颜色变换实验中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输方法主要针对二次代价（p=2），缺乏针对通用 p-代价的统一框架，且难以在灵活终端分布匹配下保证最优性。本文旨在构建一个适用于任意 p-代价的势流框架，通过自感应匹配损失实现零损失精确性，恢复广义 Benamou-Brenier 最优系统。

Method: 提出 PMOT 框架：1) 在广义 Benamou-Brenier 形式下，用标量势参数化连续归一化流（CNF）的速度场；2) 利用模型自生成的端点确定的直线桥，设计自感应匹配损失训练势梯度；3) 允许灵活的终端分布匹配（如似然、MMD 等）。

Result: 理论上证明：在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，零损失解满足广义 Benamou-Brenier 最优系统，恢复 p-最优传输映射和动力学。实验上：合成基准上学习的 p-特异映射与参考一致；高维表格数据上作为似然密度模型具有竞争力；基于 MMD 的颜色变换实验展示了灵活的基于样本的终端匹配能力。

Conclusion: PMOT 为通用 p-代价最优传输提供了一个理论可靠、实践有效的势流框架，通过自感应匹配损失实现零损失精确性，兼具密度建模和灵活分布匹配能力，拓展了最优传输在机器学习中的应用范围。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [188] [When Does Consensus Mean Correctness? Measuring the Agreement-Accuracy Coupling with Semantics-Preserving Re-Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05670)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 RENDEQ，利用科学绘图的程序化重渲染构建语义等价且答案唯一的渲染等价集，直接测量模型跨扰动一致性与正确性的耦合。在三个开放权重 VLM 上实验表明：重渲染优于重采样；一致性优于 token log-prob 基线；绘图库是主要离散因子；在自一致性上微调反而降低准确率，揭示一致性仅在错误分布足够分散时才指示正确性，且奖励一致性的目标会破坏这种分散性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作常假设自然图像扰动保留语义且以一致性作为无标签可靠性信号或自训练目标，但缺乏直接测量一致性与正确性耦合的基准：自然图像无精确答案键，语义等价仅靠假设。科学绘图由程序生成，重渲染天然语义等价且拥有程序级精确答案，消除了上述两大障碍，为直接量化耦合提供理想试验场。

Method: 构建 RENDEQ 生成器，从科学论文提取绘图代码与数据，通过改变绘图库、主题、随机种子等风格因子重渲染生成渲染等价集。在三个开放权重 VLM 上，对每个实验发现进行三次独立复现。测量重渲染与重采样的准确率、可靠性；对比一致性与 mean token log-prob 基线；分解风格因子对离散度的贡献；在模型自身跨渲染共识上微调并评估准确率变化。

Result: 1) 重渲染在准确率和可靠性上均优于重采样。2) 一致性在两个模型上优于 mean token log-prob，第三个模型持平，且修复了渲染管线 bug 后结论反转。3) 离散度主要集中在绘图库因子，贡献是次大因子的两倍以上、噪声底的一个数量级。4) 在跨渲染共识上微调导致五次复现全部准确率下降，符号与自然图像自训练文献相反。5) 一致性仅当模型错误足够分散时才指示正确性；奖励一致性的目标会压缩错误分散性，从而破坏该指示作用。

Conclusion: 利用科学绘图的程序化特性可直接量化一致性-正确性耦合。实验表明一致性作为可靠性信号有效但有条件：需错误分布足够分散。直接优化一致性会破坏这一前提，导致性能下降。揭示了自一致性训练在自然图像上报告的收益可能源于错误分布的隐性分散，而非一致性本身的因果作用。

Abstract: A model's agreement across perturbed inputs is used both as a label-free reliability signal and as a self-training target, on the premise that agreement tracks correctness. That coupling is rarely measured directly: natural-image perturbations preserve meaning only by assumption, and no exact answer key localizes errors. Scientific figures remove both obstacles, a figure is drawn from data by a program, so redrawing it yields images that are semantically equivalent by construction and share a programmatically exact answer. We build RENDEQ, a generator of such render-equivalence sets, and measure the coupling on three open-weight VLMs, checking every finding across three independent instantiations. Re-rendering beats resampling on both accuracy and reliability. Agreement beats an evidence-carrying baseline, mean token log-probability, on two of three models and ties on the third, reversing an intermediate, buggy replication traced to a rendering-pipeline failure. The dispersion behind this is concentrated in one style factor, the plotting library, more than double the next-largest factor and an order of magnitude above the noise floor. Fine-tuning on the model's own cross-render consensus inverts: accuracy falls in every one of five replication runs, the opposite sign to published results on natural images. Agreement certifies correctness only above a threshold set by how diffuse a model's errors are, and an objective that rewards agreement destroys exactly that diffuseness.

</details>


### [189] [Consistency Has a Computable Blind Spot: A Commutation Theory of Label-Free Reliability for Vision-Language Figure Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05675)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于等变性的无标签可靠性评估方法，通过不变性与等变性的互补关系，构建了Equivariance-Consistency Score，并发布REND-EQUIV数据集，实验验证了理论预测的排序，并揭示了可检测性是关系与故障类的联合属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不变性的无标签可靠性评估存在盲点：系统性误读能通过扰动测试并被错误认证为正确。作者旨在通过引入等变性关系，弥补不变性方法的不足，提供可计算的误差表征，并开发无需标签和训练的检测器。

Method: 理论分析表明，误差对编辑不可见当且仅当二者可交换，不可达误差构成联合中心化子。提出利用等变性：编辑图表数据后，正确答案应按可计算量变化。两个匹配的编辑对仿射读取错误可证明完备；循环重标记缩小标签排列的完备性缺口。实例化为Equivariance-Consistency Score，发布REND-EQUIV数据集（配对的不变性与等变性编辑集）。

Result: 在三个模型和人工标注群体上，预测的排序成立；第二不变性族方法确认盲点属于关系本身而非实现；循环重标记在真实样本上实现预测增益。相同表征解释了分类器变形测试文献中报道的排序反转：可检测性是关系与故障类的联合属性，而非仅由关系决定。

Conclusion: 论文建立了基于等变性的无标签可靠性评估理论框架，证明了不变性方法的固有盲点可通过等变性弥补，提出了可计算的检测指标和数据集，实验验证了理论预测，并揭示了可检测性的本质是关系与故障类的联合属性，为视觉语言模型的可靠性评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Label-free reliability for vision-language models rests on invariance: perturb the input and a faithful reader's answer should not change. This has a known blind spot, a systematic misreading survives the perturbation and gets certified wrong, which we show is computable, not just real: an error is invisible to an edit exactly when the two commute, so the errors a suite cannot reach form its joint centralizer, a set that shrinks as edits are added and can be written down rather than guessed at. We act on the complementary relation, equivariance: edit a figure's data and the correct answer must change by a computable amount. Two matched edits are provably complete for affine reading errors; no suite of swap edits is complete for label permutations, and cyclic relabeling closes most of that gap. We instantiate the theory as the Equivariance-Consistency Score, a label-free, training-free detector, and release REND-EQUIV, pairing matched invariance and equivariance sets over identical data. The predicted ordering holds across three models and a hand-labeled population immune to the one circularity in how it is selected; a second invariance-family method confirms the blind spot belongs to the relation, not to any implementation; and cyclic relabeling delivers its predicted gain on a matched real sample. The same characterization explains a reported inversion of this ordering in the classifier metamorphic-testing literature: detectability is a joint property of the relation and the fault class, never of the relation alone.

</details>


### [190] [SEAM: Global consistency beyond local accuracy in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05702)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SEAM框架，解决科学机器学习中局部解释能否组装为全局一致解释的问题，通过解释层将局部解释在重叠区域比较，生成通道分辨的阻塞，定位不一致并测试竞争性解释，实现全局解释一致性审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习通常在子域、基准分割或单次预测解释层面验证模型，但这种局部检查无法确保局部解释能组装成全局可接受的解释。需要一种方法使局部到全局的一致性问题可计算，跨区域、传感器、机制和模型组件检测不一致。

Method: 提出生成器无关的科学解释可容许性机器（SEAM）。有限解释层实例化SEAM-Ω为每个区域配备结构化解释（状态、闭包、观测通道及可选契约元数据），在重叠区域比较相邻解释，将分歧转化为通道分辨的阻塞。阻塞定位不一致，通过限制每种修复仅限于某一宣称解释允许的修订来测试竞争解释。精确可行性判定保留或反驳解释；当精确修复不可用时，残差感知正则化记录提供单独标记的经验归因。框架还将不一致与不可辨识性分离，并监控分布偏移下的学习生成器。建立了最小成本干预和守恒契约可检测性定理，以及可辨识性和闭包可恢复性的伴随结果。

Result: 在合成偏微分方程系统和分布外傅里叶神经算子（FNO）监控的19个实验中，SEAM即使在局部预测准确时也能检测到不兼容的解释，并将失败归因到特定通道和重叠区域。SEAM为现有求解器和学习模型增加了一个全局解释一致性审计，测试其局部解释是否形成连贯的科学解释。

Conclusion: SEAM提供了一个通用框架，使科学机器学习中局部解释的全局一致性可计算、可定位、可归因。通过解释层结构和阻塞分析，实现了跨区域、传感器、机制的解释一致性审计，区分了不一致与不可辨识性，并给出了理论保证。实验表明其能有效检测局部准确但全局不一致的解释，为科学机器学习模型提供了重要的可靠性验证工具。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning commonly validates models at the level of a subdomain, a benchmark split, or an explanation for one prediction. Yet such local checks cannot establish whether the resulting explanations can be assembled into one globally admissible explanation. We introduce Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines (SEAM), a generator-agnostic framework that makes this local-to-global consistency question computable across regions, sensors, regimes, and model components. The finite explanation-sheaf instantiation SEAM-$Ω$ represents each region by a structured explanation with state, closure, and observation channels together with optional contract metadata; compares neighboring explanations on their overlaps; and converts disagreement into a channel-resolved obstruction. This obstruction locates inconsistency and tests competing declared accounts by restricting each repair to the revisions that one account permits. Exact feasibility refutes or retains an account; when exact repair is unavailable, residual-aware regularized records provide a separately labeled empirical attribution. The framework also separates inconsistency from non-identifiability and monitors learned generators under distribution shift. We establish theorems for minimum-cost intervention and conservation-contract detectability, together with companion results for identifiability and closure recoverability. Across nineteen experiments involving synthetic partial differential equation systems and out-of-distribution Fourier neural operator (FNO) monitoring, SEAM detects incompatible explanations even when local predictions are accurate, and attributes failures to specific channels and overlaps. SEAM adds a global explanation-consistency audit to existing solvers and learning models, testing whether their local explanations form a coherent scientific account.

</details>


### [191] [GROM: Gradient-Free Rapid One-Shot Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05783)
*Paweł Batorski,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GROM提出了一种一次性机器遗忘方法，通过岭正则化最小二乘优化的闭式解，在几秒钟内完成权重编辑，实现最先进的遗忘-效用权衡，并抵抗量化攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的机器遗忘方法依赖迭代微调，计算开销大，缺乏显式解析公式，且遗忘的知识可能只是被隐藏而非移除，量化后会恢复。

Method: 将遗忘过程建模为岭正则化最小二乘优化问题，推导目标权重矩阵的闭式加性更新，仅通过无梯度前向传播计算，无需反向传播和迭代收敛。

Result: 在TOFU-5%、TOFU-10%、MUSE-Books、MUSE-News和WMDP基准上达到最先进的遗忘-效用权衡，计算开销显著降低，且能抵抗低比特量化攻击恢复被遗忘内容。

Conclusion: GROM提供了一种高效、精确的解析解遗忘方法，真正从权重中移除目标内容而非掩盖，实现了快速、鲁棒的机器遗忘。

Abstract: Machine unlearning has become a critical capability for safely removing specific, sensitive knowledge from large language models (LLMs). Current state-of-the-art approaches primarily rely on iterative, training-time unlearning via fine-tuning. However, even when utilizing parameter-efficient dimensionality reduction techniques like LoRA, gradient-based optimization remains computationally expensive and lacks explicit analytical formulations. It can also leave the targeted knowledge merely hidden rather than removed, to the point that simply quantizing the unlearned model restores much of what it was supposed to have erased. To resolve this, we propose a novel one-shot unlearning approach, abandoning iterative optimization in favor of a direct, exact analytical solution. We frame the unlearning process as a ridge-regularized least-squares optimization problem, deriving a closed-form additive update for targeted weight matrices. This update forces the selected layer to suppress unwanted content while strictly preserving its behavior on retained data. Computed from gradient-free forward passes alone, with no backpropagation and no iteration to convergence, GROM applies the weight edit in mere seconds, which makes it orders of magnitude faster than traditional fine-tuning. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that GROM achieves state-of-the-art forgetting-utility trade-offs on TOFU-5%, TOFU-10%, MUSE-Books, MUSE-News and WMDP, significantly reducing computational overhead without sacrificing overall model performance. Because the update removes the targeted content from the weights instead of masking it, GROM also withstands the low-bit quantization attack that recovers much of the content a gradient-based baseline had appeared to forget. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/GROM.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Feature Importance: A Comparative Analysis of Pattern Detection Methods in Cluster Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05880)
*Benjamin Connor,Anna Jurek-Loughrey,Lu Bai,Muhammad Fahim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了三种常用事后解释方法（随机森林代理模型、LIME、PCA）在聚类结果模式检测中的表现，发现它们虽能恢复相关特征，但无法一致检测所有注入模式类型，揭示了现有可解释性工具与聚类模式级解释需求之间的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类结果的解释仍是数据分析的根本挑战，尤其在医疗等高维数据领域需要提取有意义模式。现有可解释性技术主要关注特征重要性或局部实例级解释，而非识别簇内结构化模式。

Method: 引入一套系统注入预定义模式的合成数据集，对比评估三种广泛使用的事后分析方法：带排列特征重要性的随机森林代理模型、LIME 和主成分分析。

Result: 每种方法都能成功恢复相关特征，但没有一种方法能一致检测所有注入的模式类型。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了现有可解释性工具与聚类模式级解释需求之间的关键鸿沟，激励开发专门的模式检测方法论。

Abstract: Interpreting clustering outcomes remains a fundamental challenge in data analysis, particularly in domains such as healthcare where meaningful patterns must be extracted from high-dimensional data. While numerous explainability techniques exist, they are primarily designed to assess feature importance or provide local instance-level explanations rather than to identify structured patterns present within clusters. This work presents a comparative evaluation of commonly used post-hoc analysis methods for pattern detection in clustering results. To enable controlled evaluation, we introduce a suite of synthetic datasets in which predefined patterns are systematically injected. Three widely used techniques are evaluated: a Random Forest surrogate model with permutation feature importance, LIME (Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations), and principal component analysis. Results demonstrate that although each method can successfully recover relevant features, none consistently detects all injected pattern types. These findings high- light a critical gap between existing explainability tools and the requirements of pattern-level cluster interpretation, motivating the development of dedicated pattern detection methodologies.

</details>


### [193] [How Far Do Simple Transformations Translate Across Text Embedding Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05980)
*Sid Ali Hamideche,Louis Adrien Dufrene,Quentin Lampin,Guillaume Larue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究简单变换能否在异构文本嵌入模型间翻译表示；发现简单映射仅在部分兼容模型对间有效，兼容性取决于架构、训练目标、池化策略和数据分布，异构嵌入空间并非普遍通过简单映射相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解独立训练的模型如何组织语义信息，以实现无需解码为人类可读文本的AI到AI潜在通信；测试文献中关于潜在普遍性的假设在真实文本设置下的成立性。

Method: 聚焦轻量级翻译器（如线性映射），在九个架构、池化策略、训练目标各异的嵌入模型上，使用CKA、下游迁移、保真度和检索评估兼容性。

Result: 简单翻译器能恢复部分兼容模型对的有意义共享结构并支持迁移，但对其他模型对失效；兼容性联合取决于架构、训练目标、池化和数据分布。

Conclusion: 异构嵌入空间并非如部分文献所暗示那样普遍通过简单映射相关联。

Abstract: We investigate whether simple transformations can translate representations across heterogeneous text embedding models. Understanding how independently trained models organize semantic information is an enabler for AI-to-AI latent communication without decoding into human-readable text. Focusing on lightweight translators such as linear mappings, we test the literature hypothesis of latent universality in a realistic text setting beyond simplified benchmarks. Across nine embedding models differing in architecture, pooling strategy, and training objective, we evaluate compatibility using CKA, downstream transfer, fidelity, and retrieval. Simple translators recover meaningful shared structure and support transfer for some compatible pairs, but fail sharply for others. Compatibility depends jointly on architecture, training objective, pooling, and data distribution. Overall, the results show that heterogeneous embedding spaces are not universally related by simple mappings as often suggested in some literature.

</details>


### [194] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Agree with Themselves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06004)
*Christian Klötergens,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Tom Hanika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出表格基础模型(TFMs)在自回归多元预测中违反边缘化一致性和因子分解一致性，揭示其联合分布预测的不可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFMs 虽然在表格预测中表现优异，但其自回归多元预测生成的联合分布的忠实性未被研究，且无法在真实数据上与真实后验分布对比，因此需要新的评估标准。

Method: 提出两个一致性要求：边缘化一致性（边缘化条件分布等于直接预测的边缘分布）和因子分解一致性（不同因子分解顺序产生相同的联合分布），并对多个 TFMs 在分类和回归任务上进行评估。

Result: 所有评估的 TFMs 在所有数据集上均同时违反边缘化一致性和因子分解一致性，无论分类还是回归任务。

Conclusion: 现有 TFMs 的自回归多元预测无法保证生成有效的联合分布，亟需改进以满足一致性要求。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) are currently the best approach to tabular prediction problems. They are constructed as transformers that approximate the Bayesian posterior predictive distribution based on a pre-training prior. These univariate predictors can be converted into multivariate ones autoregressively by sampling one target and adding it to the features.
  However, the faithfulness of the resulting joint has not been investigated. Furthermore, TFMs cannot be evaluated against the posterior itself, at least not on real-world datasets, because the ground-truth distribution is unknown. We therefore propose asking a different question: could a model's predictions result from any joint distribution? To answer this question, we pose two requirements that any such model must satisfy. The first is marginalization consistency, which demands that marginalized conditionals are equal to directly predicted marginals. The second is factorization consistency, which demands that different factorization orders result in equal joint distributions. Every TFM that we evaluate violates both of these requirements for both classification and regression across all datasets.

</details>


### [195] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过对比线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励和带有潜在上下文变量的元逆强化学习模型，在3,186条北极航运AIS轨迹上评估奖励模型性能。结果显示非线性奖励显著优于线性基线，但引入船舶特定的潜在上下文反而降低性能，表明观测到的航路和环境特征已足以解释行为差异，无需引入隐式船舶偏好。同时发现不同评价指标（预测精度、航路保真度、奖励迁移）给出的模型排名不一，单一指标不足以评估学习到的奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助导航需要可解释且对环境变化鲁棒的奖励模型。逆强化学习（IRL）可从船舶轨迹中恢复奖励，元IRL引入潜在上下文变量捕捉行为异质性，但尚不清楚这些潜在表示是真正恢复了隐藏偏好，还是仅重新编码了观测状态中已有的信息。需通过受控实验验证观测特征是否已足以解释行为差异，从而指导更可信赖的AI部署。

Method: 在3,186条来自202艘船舶、跨越九个北极航运季节的AIS衍生航次上，对比三种模型：线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励（相同神经网络架构）和基于相同非线性架构的带船舶特定潜在上下文的元IRL模型。使用预留似然、行为分析、上下文探针、预注册的特征隐藏消融实验，以及预测精度、航路保真度、奖励迁移等多指标评估。

Result: 非线性共享奖励较线性基线提升预留似然50.9%；引入船舶特定潜在上下文反而使性能下降16.5%。行为分析、上下文探针和特征隐藏消融实验表明，船舶层面的表观变异主要由可观测的航路和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特有因素。预测精度、航路保真度和奖励迁移三个指标给出的模型排名不一致，说明单一指标不足以评估学习到的奖励。

Conclusion: 在引入每船舶潜在上下文前，应先测试观测到的航路、环境和船舶特征是否已解释行为变异。研究支持在安全关键领域部署更可信赖的AI，强调奖励模型评估需多指标综合考量，避免过度依赖潜在变量建模隐式偏好。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [196] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kastor方法，将确定性物理基础模型转化为高效准确的生成式代理模型，通过两阶段推理、均值预测正则化(MPR)和空间梯度匹配，在The Well基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归机器学习模拟器在长时程预测中误差累积严重，且难以捕捉复杂物理系统的随机性，需要一种能同时保证长时程准确性、随机性建模能力和计算效率的生成式代理模型。

Method: 1. 两阶段推理：大步长因果自回归模型 + 非因果时间超分辨率网络，减少误差累积并降低计算成本。2. 均值预测正则化(MPR)：约束生成模型在零噪声条件下预测确定性分布均值，提升FGN和扩散模型的性能稳定性。3. 空间梯度匹配：提高功率谱密度测量下的准确性和物理保真度。

Result: 在The Well基准的10个数据集上，相比Walrus微调基线，预测误差平均降低42.9%，在8个数据集上VRMSE优于Walrus，同时在预测精度、谱一致性和计算效率上均超越竞争方法。

Conclusion: Kastor通过创新的两阶段推理架构、MPR正则化和空间梯度匹配，成功将确定性物理基础模型转化为高效准确的生成式代理，解决了长时程误差累积和随机性建模难题，为物理模拟加速提供了新范式。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [197] [Is Self-Pretraining really useful to improve diagnosis in medical Time Series?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06122)
*Omar Coser,Antonio Orvieto,Paolo Soda,Loredana Zollo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨自监督预训练（SPT）在医疗时间序列任务中对Transformer模型的提升，发现SPT能持续提升分类准确率（0-6个百分点），尤其在深层模型和单变量输入中有效，且无需任务特定架构修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Transformer在长上下文基准上受益于自监督预训练的启发，研究旨在评估SPT在多模态、多变量及单变量医疗时间序列任务中的泛化能力和可扩展性，特别是在数据有限的临床场景下。

Method: 在三个医疗时间序列数据集（康复机器人、压力检测、帕金森步态检测）上评估Transformer架构，对比从头训练与基于四种掩码目标的SPT预训练，并系统变化模型深度以考察容量与预训练收益的交互。

Result: SPT在所有数据集和配置上均能提升分类准确率（0-6个百分点），增益随模型深度增加而增大，单变量输入也能受益，掩码策略、数据集和架构影响增益大小。

Conclusion: SPT是一种简单通用的策略，能在不改变任务特定架构的情况下提升Transformer在医疗时间序列任务上的性能，有望增强数据受限临床环境下的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Inspired by recent evidence that transformer architectures benefit from Self-PreTraining (SPT) on long-context benchmarks, we investigate whether similar gains extend to multimodal, multivariate, and even simple univariate medical time series. Our objective is to assess the impact of SPT on the performance and scalability of transformer-based models across diverse medical applications, particularly under limited data conditions. We evaluate transformer architectures on three representative medical time-series tasks: rehabilitation robotics (Camargo dataset), stress detection (Non-EEG Stress), and Parkinson's disease detection (Gait Parkinson's Disease). Models are trained either from scratch or through SPT using four masking-based objectives designed to promote temporal and cross-modal representation learning, and we systematically vary model depth to examine how capacity interacts with pre-training benefits. Across datasets and configurations, SPT consistently improves classification accuracy by 0-6 percentage points depending on masking strategy, dataset and architecture, with gains observed not only in multivariate settings but also when models are restricted to simple univariate inputs. The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [198] [LLM Inference Under Bursty Workload Distribution: Modifying the WAIT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06135)
*Anjali Gangadhar Katageria,Shobha Rani,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种轻量级扩展算法，基于现有的WAIT算法，通过在线估计请求到达强度来适应时变的流量模式，解决了现有调度算法假设泊松恒定到达率而忽略真实流量突发性的问题。在基于MMPP的合成工作负载仿真中，该方法在低到达率变化场景下比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现更高吞吐量，同时保持相当的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM推理调度算法（如Sarathi-Serve、ORCA、vLLM）通常假设请求到达服从恒定速率的泊松过程，但真实世界的LLM服务流量具有突发性和时变性，这种假设与实际不符，导致在动态流量下性能下降。

Method: 提出WAIT算法的轻量级扩展，利用观测到的请求间到达时间进行在线请求强度估计，无需先验流量知识即可适应时变到达率。使用基于马尔可夫调制泊松过程（MMPP）的合成工作负载进行仿真评估，包含多种请求类型。

Result: 在评估的低到达率变化场景中，该方法实现的吞吐量高于Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM，同时保持可比较的延迟性能。

Conclusion: 该工作通过简单的在线到达率估计机制有效解决了LLM服务中流量时变性带来的调度挑战，为实际部署中的动态流量适应提供了轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and Claude are widely used for information retrieval and problem-solving. Recent work has focused on improving scheduling algorithms to boost throughput while maintaining low latency. However, these approaches often assume Poisson request arrivals with constant rates - an assumption that fails to reflect the inherently bursty and dynamic nature of real-world traffic. We propose a lightweight extension to the state-of-the-art WAIT algorithm [1], which adapts to time-varying arrival rates without prior traffic knowledge. The proposed algorithm performs online estimation of request intensity based on observed interarrival times. Using Markov Modulated Poisson Process (MMPP)-based synthetic workloads with diverse request types, we conduct a simulation-based evaluation demonstrating that the proposed method achieves higher throughput than Sarathi-Serve [2], ORCA [3], and vLLM [4] in the evaluated low arrival-rate shift scenarios while maintaining comparable latency.

</details>


### [199] [SkillTFM: Gated Skill Evolution for Training-Free Adaptation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06137)
*Yi He,Zhengkang Guan,Anpeng Wu,Peng Cui,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillTFM，一种无需训练的表格基础模型（TFM）适应系统，通过可验证且可扩展的技能库，结合边界证据识别与门控技能演化，在模拟边界设置和真实电价预测任务上显著提升AUC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）虽为通用表格学习提供了可复用预测器，但实际部署受限于分布偏移、异构特征语义和任务特定模式，难以在无需昂贵微调或额外标注数据的情况下捕捉。

Method: SkillTFM将TFM适应从参数更新转移到门控技能演化。核心是可验证且可扩展的技能库，耦合边界证据识别（表征任务结构和基础模型失效模式）与门控技能演化（检索并扩展可复用技能，并进行显式验证）。

Result: 在模拟边界设置和真实电价预测中，SkillTFM将AUC提升0.128-0.142，非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898。跨TFM主干网络实验证明其有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: SkillTFM通过无训练的技能库机制，有效解决了TFM在分布偏移和任务特定模式下的适应难题，为表格基础模型的实际部署提供了通用且高效的适应方案。

Abstract: Tabular data are ubiquitous in real-world applications and are crucial for data-driven prediction and decision-making across science, industry, finance, healthcare, and public services. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose tabular learning, offering reusable predictors across diverse datasets and substantially reducing the need for task-specific training, tuning, and model development. However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [200] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种后训练早停机制，利用二值神经网络中累积和提前偏离零的特性，在不重新训练参数的情况下预测最终符号，从而减少累加操作。在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深卷积层累加项减少86.6%，准确率仅下降0.37%；三层最深卷积同时应用可减少全网25%算术运算，准确率下降1.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络虽适合受限部署，但二值激活下的点积仍需累加所有输入，仅保留符号，造成大量冗余计算。观察到运行时部分和常提前远离零，最终符号可预测，因此可通过早停减少累加操作。

Method: 后训练早停机制：在训练数据集上表征运行时累积行为，利用该信息尽早预测最终符号，无需重新训练模型参数。在理想化权重排序下统计操作数。

Result: VGG11/CIFAR-10实验：最深卷积层累加项减少86.6%，准确率下降0.37%；三层最深卷积同时应用，全网算术运算减少25%，准确率下降1.36%。

Conclusion: 提出的早停机制能显著降低二值神经网络推理计算量，且精度损失可控，为受限环境部署提供有效优化手段。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [201] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGA框架，利用依存句法分析器监督替代人工偏好标注，通过delta-DPO实现低资源语言的语法对齐，在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威语博克马尔语上显著提升语法质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化虽有效但依赖昂贵的人工偏好标注，对于形态丰富的低资源语言尤为困难，因为这类语言缺乏高质量标注数据。

Method: SAGA将解析器判断转化为delta-DPO的偏好对，结合解析器质量与词汇多样性构建复合奖励，采用奖励间隙标准过滤低信息量对，并监控奖励作弊以保持监督可靠性。

Result: 在GPT-SW3-1.3B上：丹麦语解析成功率从69.0%提升至93.8%；冰岛语在独立Stanza评估上提升4.5个百分点（三次运行平均+3.3个百分点），母语人员在80%的成对比较中偏好SAGA输出；挪威语博克马尔语提升28个百分点。

Conclusion: 在拥有高质量依存句法分析器的低资源语言中，解析器衍生的监督信号是人工偏好标注的实用替代方案，可有效实现语法对齐。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [202] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个三维框架（When、How、Where）来刻画持续学习从以参数为中心向系统级适应的范式转移，系统综述了代表性方法并探讨了挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习主要关注参数中心的机制（训练策略、架构设计、权重适应），但新兴范式（如在线策略学习、测试时训练、外部记忆/技能库/交互协议）正在将持续学习的边界扩展到静态参数空间之外，亟需一个统一框架来刻画这种从参数中心向系统级适应的转变。

Method: 构建三维分析框架：How 维度涵盖离线策略、在线策略及超越梯度的优化机制；When 维度捕捉预训练、后训练、推理时三个阶段的演进；Where 维度划分内部参数更新与外部结构约束。在此框架下系统调研代表性方法，梳理演进脉络。

Result: 梳理了持续学习在三个维度上的演进路径，揭示了从单一参数更新向系统级适应的转变趋势，总结了当前面临的关键挑战（如评估基准、灾难性遗忘新形式、系统集成复杂度）及更广泛的影响（如智能体终身学习、人机协作）。

Conclusion: 持续学习正经历从参数中心到系统级适应的范式转移，三维框架为理解和推动该领域发展提供了结构化视角，未来研究应关注跨维度的协同机制、统一评估体系以及面向真实世界部署的系统化设计。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [203] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3 是一种概率性全球天气模型，通过显式多时间步推理解决了固定自回归时间步的权衡问题，单一权重即可在推理时配置时间步，兼顾预测性和可用性，且混合时间步训练提升滚动稳定性。模型采用轻量级邻域注意力 Transformer（约 1.34 亿参数），在赤道矩形网格上实现接近 SOTA 的中程概率预报技能、稳定的延伸范围滚动、高效的训练推理及决策相关诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型依赖预设固定的自回归时间步。较短时间步（如 1-6 小时）能精细解析日内循环动力学，但会增加给定预报时效的误差累积；较长时间步（如 24 小时）虽减少误差累积，却限制了亚日尺度高可预报性的短时预报可用性。需要一种能在推理时灵活配置时间步、平衡预测性与可用性的方法。

Method: 提出 GEM-3，采用显式多时间步推理：单一训练权重，推理时可配置模型时间步。引入混合时间步训练策略，提升滚动稳定性。架构上为轻量级邻域注意力 Transformer（约 1.34 亿参数），基于赤道矩形网格，包含超越 GEM-2 的多项架构改进。

Result: GEM-3 实现了接近最先进的中程概率预报技能、稳定的延伸范围滚动预报、高效的训练与推理，并提供决策相关的诊断输出。混合时间步训练显著优于单一时间步专家模型。

Conclusion: GEM-3 通过多时间步推理与混合时间步训练，成功解决了固定时间步的权衡，构建了一个实用、高效且技能强劲的概率性全球天气预报系统，为从短时到延伸范围的多尺度预报提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [204] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了训练后适配的文献，提出六维分类法（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型），澄清微调、检索增强、提示等易混淆概念，揭示从传统机器学习到多模态大语言模型的适配策略演进，映射技术间的继承、取代、混合与分层部署关系，并指出评估、复现、持久推理时适配、遗忘、多模态适配及治理感知工作流等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练后适配文献在技术族系、模型类别、部署场景间碎片化，难以对比方法或描述模型修改过程，亟需统一的分类框架与术语体系以支撑技术文档、模型变更追踪与治理分析。

Method: 通过文献综述与概念综合，构建六维分类法（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围、模型类型），区分易混淆术语，追溯从传统机器学习、深度学习、基础模型到大语言模型及多模态大语言模型的适配策略演进，梳理技术间的继承、取代、混合与分层部署关系。

Result: 形成统一的术语词表与分类体系，可用于技术文档编写、模型变更记录与治理合规分析；明确了微调、检索增强、提示等核心概念的边界；揭示了适配技术在不同模型演进阶段的演变脉络与组合模式。

Conclusion: 训练后适配已成现代机器学习核心实践，六维分类法提供了结构化理解框架；未来需重点攻克评估基准统一、复现性保障、持久推理时适配、高效遗忘、多模态适配融合以及治理感知的训练后工作流等挑战。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [205] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开发了专门针对代谢组学的大语言模型MetaboLLM和基于图同构网络的MetaboLLM-GIN，将生化知识转化为可预测的代谢物图表示，在应激性高血糖预测和绝经后激素方案分类任务中取得最优AUC，并产生生物学意义的解释性发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在异构资源中，难以转化为预测性表示，需要领域专用语言模型来整合异构生化知识并构建可解释的代谢物图表示。

Method: 开发MetaboLLM，通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索适配代谢组学领域；开发MetaboLLM-GIN，将生成的生化描述转化为代谢物图，利用图同构网络进行患者级预测；在四个骨干模型家族上评估，并在外部公共基准上迁移测试。

Result: MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上优于基础模型和医学适配模型，并能迁移到外部基准。MetaboLLM-GIN在应激性高血糖预测（AUC 0.8616）和绝经后激素方案分类（AUC 0.8123）中取得最高AUC，优于传统模型、替代图构建方法及未适配/非检索LLM配置生成的图。模型解释产生生物学意义的发现。

Conclusion: 领域专用语言模型能将异构生化知识组织为具有预测性和可解释性的代谢物图表示，为代谢组学临床应用提供新范式。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [206] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了反应对比学习基础模型（RxnCLF），基于凝聚反应图（CRG）的自监督对比学习框架，在170万Pistachio反应上预训练，微调后在多个收率预测基准上超越现有图/序列基线，展示了作为可扩展反应基础模型的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应收率预测面临标注数据稀缺、反应空间组合巨大且稀疏的挑战；现有基于字符串、指纹、图的反应编码仅部分捕捉化学转化，难以准确预测复杂底物反应。

Method: 构建凝聚反应图（CRG）将反应物和产物信息统一到单一图中，设计自监督对比学习框架RxnCLF，在170万Pistachio反应上预训练，学习紧凑连续的潜在空间，捕捉反应中心特征和侧链上下文，随后在多个收率预测基准上微调。

Result: RxnCLF在Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化BH偶联、专有HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成数据集上持续优于图和序列基线，提升R2并达到整体最佳性能。

Conclusion: 基于CRG的RxnCLF作为可扩展反应基础模型展现前景，有望泛化至更广反应空间，支持区域选择性、对映选择性预测及反应条件优化等下游任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [207] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在条件查询模型下，自适应测试与非自适应测试的查询复杂度差距。主要结果：可学习性当且仅当两个分布类在成对条件概率上具有正分离度；零分离度时最优最坏情况误差恒为1/2。任意T查询自适应策略可用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))非自适应配对查询模拟，总变分距离≤ρ。存在常数自适应查询复杂度但需Ω_ε(N²)非自适应查询的分布族，最坏情况固定误差自适应性差距为Θ_ε(N²)。交互可将所需测试数二次方级降低，但指数级分支不带来指数级查询优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在理解模型评估中自适应查询（根据先前响应选择后续测试）与非自适应查询（预先固定所有测试）的本质区别。在有限结果空间的条件查询模型下，研究两个核心问题：(1) 哪些分布类对可以被可靠区分？(2) 当所有查询必须预先固定时，需要多少额外查询才能匹配自适应测试器的性能？

Method: 采用条件查询模型，在大小为N的有限结果空间上分析。理论方法包括：(1) 通过成对条件概率的分离度表征可学习性；(2) 构造随机非自适应过程模拟自适应策略，使用配对查询并预先选定所有查询；(3) 通过构造匹配分布族建立下界，证明自适应查询复杂度为常数而非自适应需Ω_ε(N²)；(4) 使用总变分距离度量模拟 transcript 的近似程度。

Result: 1. 可学习性当且仅当两类分布在成对条件概率上具有正分离度；零分离度时，任何有限查询预算下的最优最坏情况误差恰为1/2。2. 对任意T查询自适应策略和ρ∈(0,1)，存在随机非自适应过程，使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))配对查询，其模拟 transcript 与自适应 transcript 在总变分距离上≤ρ，一致对所有模型分布成立。3. 存在分布族具有常数自适应查询复杂度但需要Ω_ε(N²)非自适应查询复杂度。4. 最坏情况固定误差自适应性差距为Θ_ε(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互式评估可将所需测试数量减少二次方因子（Θ(N²)），但自适应查询的指数级分支结构并不带来指数级的查询优势。自适应性的根本优势在于能根据中间结果聚焦查询，而在最坏情况下这种优势仅表现为多项式（平方级）的查询复杂度节省。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [208] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG
